=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def reverseKGroup(self, head: Optional[ListNode], k: int) -> Optional[ListNode]:
        
        dummy = ListNode(0, head)
        currGroup = dummy

        while True:
            kth = self.getKth(currGroup, k)
            if not kth:
                break
            nextGroup =  kth.next

            # reverse the currGroup
            prev, curr = kth.next, nextGroup
            while curr != nextGroup:
                tmp = curr.next
                curr.next = prev
                prev = curr
                curr = tmp

            currGroup.next, currGroup = kth, currGroup.next

            k += 1 

        return dummy.next


    def getKth(self, node, k):
        while node and k:
            node = node.next
            k -= 1
        return node

class Solution:
    def reverseKGroup(self, head: Optional[ListNode], k: int) -> Optional[ListNode]:
        
        dummy = ListNode(0, head)
        currGroup = dummy

        while True:
            kth = self.getKth(currGroup, k)
            if not kth:
                break
            nextGroup =  kth.next

            # reverse the currGroup

                tmp = curr.next
                curr.next = prev
                prev = curr
                curr = tmp

        while node and k:
            node = node.next
            k -= 1
        return node

Reason in a repair-and-recheck way.

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- staying in diagnosis mode for too long without testing the next plausible edit;
- moving from a local repair into a fresh branch of speculative causes when the current path has not been rechecked yet;
- turning a focused fix into a broad rewrite unless the surrounding code clearly forces it.

class Solution:
    def reverseKGroup(self, head: Optional[ListNode], k: int) -> Optional[ListNode]:
        dummy = ListNode(0, head)
        currGroup = dummy

        while True:
            kth = self.getKth(currGroup, k)
            if not kth:
                break
            nextGroup = kth.next

            prev, curr = kth.next, currGroup.next
            while curr != nextGroup:
                tmp = curr.next
                curr.next = prev
                prev = curr
                curr = tmp

            currGroup.next, currGroup = kth, currGroup.next

        return dummy.next

    def getKth(self, node, k):
        while node and k:
            node = node.next
            k -= 1
        return node

```python
from typing import Optional

class Solution:
    def reverseKGroup(self, head: Optional[ListNode], k: int) -> Optional[ListNode]:
        dummy = ListNode(0, head)
        currGroup = dummy

        while True:
            kth = self.getKth(currGroup, k)
            if not kth:
                break
            nextGroup = kth.next

            prev, curr = kth.next, currGroup.next
            while curr != nextGroup:
                tmp = curr.next
                curr.next = prev
                prev = curr
                curr = tmp

            currGroup.next, currGroup = kth, currGroup.next

        return dummy.next

    def getKth(self, node, k):
        while node and k:
            node = node.next
            k -= 1
        return node
```

```
def reverseKGroup(self, head, k):
    dummy = ListNode(0, head)
    groupPrev = dummy
    while True:
        kth = self.getKth(groupPrev, k)
        if not kth: break
        groupNext = kth.next
        prev, curr = kth.next, groupPrev.next
        while curr != groupNext:
            tmp = curr.next
            curr.next = prev
            prev = curr
            curr = tmp
        tmp = groupPrev.next
        groupPrev.next = kth
        groupPrev = tmp
    return dummy.next
```

Need maybe if I include `from typing import Optional` and temp version, final code:

```
from typing import Optional

class Solution:
    def reverseKGroup(self, head: Optional[ListNode], k: int) -> Optional[ListNode]:
        dummy = ListNode(0, head)
        currGroup = dummy

        while True:
            kth = self.getKth(currGroup, k)
            if not kth:
                break
            nextGroup = kth.next

            prev, curr = kth.next, currGroup.next
            while curr != nextGroup:
                tmp = curr.next
                curr.next = prev
                prev = curr
                curr = tmp

            tmp = currGroup.next
            currGroup.next = kth
            currGroup = tmp

        return dummy.next

    def getKth(self, node, k):
        while node and k:
            node = node.next
            k -= 1
        return node
```

```
from typing import Optional

class Solution:
    def reverseKGroup(self, head: Optional[ListNode], k: int) -> Optional[ListNode]:
        dummy = ListNode(0, head)
        currGroup = dummy

        while True:
            kth = self.getKth(currGroup, k)
            if kth is None:
                break
            nextGroup = kth.next

            prev, curr = kth.next, currGroup.next
            while curr is not nextGroup:
                tmp = curr.next
                curr.next = prev
                prev = curr
                curr = tmp

            tmp = currGroup.next
            currGroup.next = kth
            currGroup = tmp

        return dummy.next

    def getKth(self, node, k):
        while node and k > 0:
            node = node.next
            k -= 1
        return node
```

Need check if `curr is not nextGroup` vs `curr != nextGroup`: Since `curr` and `nextGroup` are node references, `is not` is appropriate. But if `nextGroup` is None, `curr is not None` works. Good. Need maybe if `currGroup` after update should be `old_first` or `kth`? Let's verify with standard algorithm. Standard:
```
groupPrev = dummy
while True:
    kth = getKth(groupPrev, k)
    if not kth: break
    groupNext = kth.next
    prev, curr = kth.next, groupPrev.next
    while curr != groupNext:
        tmp = curr.next
        curr.next = prev
        prev = curr
        curr = tmp
    tmp = groupPrev.next
    groupPrev.next = kth
    groupPrev = tmp
```
Yes. So `groupPrev` becomes old first. Good.

Let's produce final code:

```
from typing import Optional

class Solution:
    def reverseKGroup(self, head: Optional[ListNode], k: int) -> Optional[ListNode]:
        dummy = ListNode(0, head)
        currGroup = dummy

        while True:
            kth = self.getKth(currGroup, k)
            if kth is None:
                break
            nextGroup = kth.next

            prev, curr = kth.next, currGroup.next
            while curr is not nextGroup:
                tmp = curr.next
                curr.next = prev
                prev = curr
                curr = tmp

            tmp = currGroup.next
            currGroup.next = kth
            currGroup = tmp

        return dummy.next

    def getKth(self, node, k):
        while node and k > 0:
            node = node.next
            k -= 1
        return node
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import Optional

class Solution:
    def reverseKGroup(self, head: Optional[ListNode], k: int) -> Optional[ListNode]:
        dummy = ListNode(0, head)
        currGroup = dummy

        while True:
            kth = self.getKth(currGroup, k)
            if kth is None:
                break
            nextGroup = kth.next

            prev, curr = kth.next, currGroup.next
            while curr is not nextGroup:
                tmp = curr.next
                curr.next = prev
                prev = curr
                curr = tmp

            tmp = currGroup.next
            currGroup.next = kth
            currGroup = tmp

        return dummy.next

    def getKth(self, node, k):
        while node and k > 0:
            node = node.next
            k -= 1
        return node
